TEMPLATE · STATIC RENDER PROOF

Template execution lab

The shared source is parsed before this page is built. AST mode executes the committed nodes; generated mode lowers those same nodes into host-language renderers. Both modes use the same assign, define and output contract.

Cross-language proofPASS
AST modeIMPLEMENTED
Generated mode5 LANGUAGES
Page validationHTML

01 · EVIDENCE

Same bytes from AST mode

Source parsing occurs during the build. Runtime checks load the committed artifacts, apply assign and define, and compare UTF-8 output bytes. Generated mode must produce the same bytes before it can be enabled for a language.

ScenarioBytesSHA-256tsgorustphp
compiler-coverage351f9e5af2122de…PASSPASSPASSPASS
empty-state78d98cc268d4c4…PASSPASSPASSPASS
html-slot111b1fc22f8d29c…PASSPASSPASSPASS
react-boundary1244c247742a44e…PASSPASSPASSPASS
scope-precedence1777b160d439d3d…PASSPASSPASSPASS

02 · INPUT AND OUTPUT

Small SSR situations

Each card shows the exact assign data, define map, every template file, compiled artifact manifest and final HTML.

Generated compiler coverage

One readable page executes every canonical statement and expression family through AST and generated modes.

expressionsloops and loop metadatainclude scopedefine and block scope

Inputs

assign
{
  "flag": true,
  "dangerous": "&<>\"'",
  "empty_list": [],
  "empty_map": {},
  "page": {
    "title": "Guide"
  },
  "numbers": [
    10,
    20
  ],
  "lookup": {
    "x": "X"
  },
  "rows": [
    {
      "name": "A"
    },
    {
      "name": "B"
    }
  ]
}
define
{
  "layout": "layout.tpl",
  "content": {
    "template": "card.tpl",
    "data": {
      "label": "define"
    }
  }
}
templates · parser ranges
--- card.tpl
<p class="card">{= label}</p>


--- layout.tpl
{values = [0, ...numbers]}
{merged = [...lookup, 'z' => 'Z']}
<section>
<h1>{= page.title}</h1>
<p class="escaped">{= dangerous}</p>
<p class="logical">{= flag && 'x'}|{= false || 2}</p>
<p class="empty-truthiness">{= empty_list && flag}|{= empty_map && flag}</p>
<p>{= values[1]}|{= merged['z']}</p>
{? flag && page.title == 'Guide'}<strong>matched</strong>{:}<strong>missed</strong>{/}
<p>{= flag ? 'yes' : 'no'}|{= -1 + 3}|{= '' | default('fallback')}</p>
<ul>
{@ row = rows}
<li>{= row.index_}/{= row.size_}:{= row.name}:{= row.first_}:{= row.last_}</li>
{:}
<li>empty</li>
{/}
</ul>
{+ partial.tpl}
{?# content}<p>defined</p>{:}<p>missing</p>{/}
{# content label:page.title}
</section>


--- partial.tpl
<p class="included">{= values[2]}</p>

// Generated.
import { Frame, RenderContext, RuntimeBindings, RuntimeEnvironment, Scope, bind, bindMap, type MapValue, type PreparedRender, type Program, type RenderOptions, type Template, type Value } from '@polyspec/template';
export interface Page {
  title: string;
}
export interface Row {
  name: string;
}
export interface Slot {
  template?: string;
  html?: string;
}
export interface Assign {
  flag: boolean;
  dangerous: string;
  empty_list: Array<string>;
  empty_map: Map<string, string>;
  page: Page;

function generated_bind_type(mixed $value, array $type, string $path): mixed { if ($value === null) { if (($type['optional'] ?? false) || $type['kind'] === 'null' || $type['kind'] === 'any') return null; throw new InvalidArgumentException($path .     const boundData = data === undefined ? {} : generatedBindRecord(data, spec.input, 'define.' + id + '.data', true);
    definitions[spec.field] = { template: spec.target, data: boundData };
    targets.set(id, { target: spec.target });
} return { definitions: definitions, targets }; }
function render_content_tpl(assign, definitions, input, context, runtime, rootData, scope) {

function render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, input, context, runtime, rootData, scope) {
    const frame = new Frame("layout.tpl", [0, 7, 26, 38, 46], rootData);
    context.at(frame, [0, 11]);

    context.at(frame, [11, 20]);
    context.output.write(runtime.escape(assign.title, frame, [14, 19]));
    context.at(frame, [20, 26]);

        let definition = definitions.content;
        if (definition === undefined)
            throw runtime.error(frame, [26, 37], 'E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED', "define content is not registered");

            context.at(frame, [26, 37]);
            context.output.write(definition.html);
        }

            }
            finally {
                context.leave();
            }
        }
    }
    context.at(frame, [38, 46]);
    context.output.write("</main>\n");
}
function renderTemplate(target, assign, definitions, context, runtime, rootData, scope) {
    switch (target) {
        case "content.tpl":
            render_content_tpl(assign, definitions, {}, context, runtime, rootData, scope);
            return;
        case "layout.tpl":
            render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, {}, context, runtime, rootData, scope);
            return;
        default: throw context.fail('E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND', null, null, 'template ' + target + ' does not exist');
    }
}

    throw new Error('env.timezone is not a string'); if (typeof now !== 'number')

func strconvQuote(input string) string { data, _ := json.Marshal(input); return string(data) }

function generated_bind_type(mixed $value, array $type, string $path): mixed { if ($value === null) { if (($type['optional'] ?? false) || $type['kind'] === 'null' || $type['kind'] === 'any') return null; throw new InvalidArgumentException($path .
